This is likely in:
- A task template or task description that gets created when reviews need to be re-run

- desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/
- desloppify/app/commands/review/
- desloppify/engine/_plan/
- desloppify/intelligence/

    24→def _validate_attestation(
    25→    attestation: str,
    26→    stage: str,
    27→    *,
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> str | None:
    31→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
    32→    text = attestation.lower()
    33→
    34→    if stage == "observe":
    35→        if dimensions:
    36→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    37→            if not found:
    38→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→    elif stage == "reflect":
    42→        refs: list[str] = []
    43→        if dimensions:
    44→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    45→        if cluster_names:
    46→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    47→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    48→            return (
    49→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→            )
    53→
    54→    elif stage == "organize":
    55→        if cluster_names:
    56→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    57→            if not found:
    58→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    59→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    60→
    61→    elif stage == "enrich":
    62→        if cluster_names:
    63→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    64→            if not found:
    65→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    66→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you enriched. Mention one of: {names}"
    67→
    68→    elif stage == "sense-check":
    69→        if cluster_names:
    70→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    71→            if not found:
    72→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    73→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you sense-checked. Mention one of: {names}"
    74→
    75→    return None
    76→
    77→def _confirm_observe(
    78→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    stages: dict,
    81→    attestation: str | None,
    82→    *,
    83→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    84→) -> None:
    85→    """Show observe summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
    86→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    87→    if "observe" not in stages:
    88→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: observe stage not recorded.", "red"))
    89→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    90→        return
    91→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
    92→        print(colorize("  Observe stage already confirmed.", "green"))
    93→        return
    94→
    95→    # Show summary
    96→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    97→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
    98→    obs = stages["observe"]
    99→
   100→    print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Analyse issues & spot contradictions", "bold"))
   101→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
   102→
   103→    # Dimension breakdown
   104→    by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
   105→
   106→    issue_count = obs.get("issue_count", len(si.open_issues))
   107→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {issue_count} issues across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
   108→    for dim in dim_names:
   109→        print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} issues")
   110→
   111→    cited = obs.get("cited_ids", [])
   112→    if cited:
   113→        print(f"  You cited {len(cited)} issue IDs in your report.")
   114→
   115→    # Gate: must cite a meaningful fraction of issues (skip if no review issues exist)
   116→    min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10)) if issue_count > 0 else 0
   117→    if len(cited) < min_citations:
   118→        print(colorize(
   119→            f"\n  Cannot confirm: only {len(cited)} issue ID(s) cited in report (need {min_citations}+).",
   120→            "red",
   121→        ))
   122→        print(colorize(
   123→            "  Your observe report should reference specific issues by their hash IDs to prove",
   124→            "dim",
   125→        ))
   126→        print(colorize(
   127→            "  you actually read them. Cite at least 10% of issues or 5, whichever is smaller.",
   128→            "dim",
   129→        ))
   130→        print(colorize(
   131→            "  Re-record observe with more issue citations, then re-confirm.",
   132→            "dim",
   133→        ))
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   137→        if attestation:
   138→            print(colorize(
   139→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   140→                "red",
   141→            ))
   142→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   143→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))
   144→        print(colorize("  If not, continue reviewing issues before reflecting.", "dim"))
   145→        return
   146→
   147→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   148→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
   149→    if validation_err:
   150→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    # Record confirmation
   154→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   155→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   156→    purge_triage_stage(plan, "observe")
   157→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   158→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   159→        plan,
   160→        "triage_confirm_observe",
   161→        actor="user",
   162→        detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()},
   163→    )
   164→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   165→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Observe confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
   166→    print_user_message(
   167→        "Hey — observe is confirmed. Run `desloppify plan triage"
   168→        " --stage reflect --report \"...\"` next. No need to reply,"
   169→        " just keep going."
   170→    )

   172→def _confirm_reflect(
   173→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   174→    plan: dict,
   175→    stages: dict,
   176→    attestation: str | None,
   177→    *,
   178→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   179→) -> None:
   180→    """Show reflect summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""

   185→        return

   192→    ref = stages["reflect"]
   193→
   194→    print(colorize("  Stage: REFLECT — Form strategy & present to user", "bold"))
   195→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   196→
   197→    # Recurring dimensions
   198→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(
   199→        si.open_issues,
   200→        si.resolved_issues,
   201→    )
   202→    if recurring:
   203→        print(f"  Your strategy identified {len(recurring)} recurring dimension(s):")
   204→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   205→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   206→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   207→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   208→            print(f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}")

   210→        print("  No recurring patterns detected.")
   211→
   212→    # Strategy briefing excerpt

   219→        if len(report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   220→            print(colorize("  │ ...", "cyan"))
   221→        print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 51 + "┘", "cyan"))
   222→
   223→    # Collect data references for validation — include observe-stage dimensions
   224→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
   225→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   226→    reflect_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   227→
   228→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   229→        if attestation:

   231→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   232→                "red",
   233→            ))
   234→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   235→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect --attestation "My strategy accounts for..."', "dim"))
   236→        print(colorize("  If not, refine your strategy before organizing.", "dim"))
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   240→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   241→        attestation.strip(), "reflect",
   242→        dimensions=reflect_dims, cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   243→    )
   244→    if validation_err:
   245→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   246→        return
   247→
   248→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   249→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   250→    purge_triage_stage(plan, "reflect")
   251→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   252→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   253→        plan,

   258→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)

   260→    print_user_message(

   263→        " --stage organize --report \"...\"`. No need to reply,"

   267→def _confirm_organize(

   342→    scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
   343→    if scattered:
   344→        print(colorize(f"\n  Warning: {len(scattered)} cluster(s) span many unrelated directories:", "yellow"))
   345→        for name, dir_count, sample_dirs in scattered:
   346→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {dir_count} directories — likely grouped by theme, not area", "yellow"))
   347→            for d in sample_dirs[:3]:
   348→                print(colorize(f"      {d}", "dim"))
   349→        print(colorize("  Consider splitting into area-focused clusters (same files in same PR).", "dim"))
   350→
   351→    # Advisory: high step-to-issue ratio (1:1 mapping instead of consolidation)
   352→    high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
   353→    if high_ratio:
   354→        print(colorize(f"\n  Warning: {len(high_ratio)} cluster(s) have step count ≥ issue count:", "yellow"))
   355→        for name, steps, issues, ratio in high_ratio:
   356→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {steps} steps for {issues} issues ({ratio:.1f}x)", "yellow"))
   357→        print(colorize("  Steps should consolidate changes to the same file. 1:1 means each issue is its own step.", "dim"))
   358→
   359→    # Advisory: cross-cluster file overlap warning with specific suggestions
   360→    overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
   361→    if overlaps:
   362→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   363→        print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) reference the same files:", "yellow"))
   364→        for a, b, files in overlaps[:5]:
   365→            print(colorize(f"    {a} ↔ {b}: {len(files)} shared file(s)", "yellow"))
   366→        # Suggest specific --depends-on for pairs that don't already have a dependency
   367→        needs_dep = []
   368→        for a, b, files in overlaps:
   369→            a_deps = set(clusters.get(a, {}).get("depends_on_clusters", []))
   370→            b_deps = set(clusters.get(b, {}).get("depends_on_clusters", []))
   371→            if b not in a_deps and a not in b_deps:
   372→                needs_dep.append((a, b, files))
   373→        if needs_dep:
   374→            print(colorize("  These pairs have no dependency relationship — add one to prevent merge conflicts:", "dim"))
   375→            for a, b, _files in needs_dep[:5]:
   376→                print(colorize(f"    desloppify plan cluster update {b} --depends-on {a}", "dim"))
   377→                print(colorize(f"    # or: desloppify plan cluster update {a} --depends-on {b}", "dim"))
   378→
   379→    # Advisory: dependency self-references
   380→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   381→    for cname, c in clusters.items():
   382→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   383→        if cname in deps:
   384→            print(colorize(f"  Warning: {cname} depends on itself.", "yellow"))
   385→
   386→    # Advisory: clusters with steps but no issues (may need cleanup)
   387→    orphaned = [
   388→        (n, len(c.get("action_steps", [])))
   389→        for n, c in clusters.items()
   390→        if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
   391→    ]
   392→    if orphaned:
   393→        print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(orphaned)} cluster(s) have steps but no issues:", "yellow"))
   394→        for name, step_count in orphaned:
   395→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {step_count} steps, 0 issues", "yellow"))
   396→        print(colorize("  These may need issues added, or may be leftover from resolved work.", "dim"))
   397→
   398→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   399→        if attestation:
   400→            print(colorize(
   401→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   402→                "red",
   403→            ))
   404→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   405→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize --attestation "This plan is correct..."', "dim"))
   406→        print(colorize("  If not, adjust clusters, priorities, or queue order before completing.", "dim"))
   407→        return
   408→
   409→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   410→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   411→        attestation.strip(), "organize", cluster_names=organize_clusters,
   412→    )
   413→    if validation_err:
   414→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   415→        return
   416→
   417→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(

   419→    )
   420→    stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   421→    stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   422→    purge_triage_stage(plan, "organize")

   435→    print_user_message(

   438→        " Run `desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report \"...\"`."

   442→
   443→def _confirm_enrich(
   444→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   445→    plan: dict,
   446→    stages: dict,
   447→    attestation: str | None,
   448→    *,
   449→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   450→) -> None:
   451→    """Show enrich summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   452→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   453→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   454→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
   455→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   456→        return
   457→    if stages["enrich"].get("confirmed_at"):
   458→        print(colorize("  Enrich stage already confirmed.", "green"))
   459→        return
   460→
   461→    from ._stage_validation import (
   462→        _shallow_steps,
   463→        _steps_missing_issue_refs,
   464→        _steps_referencing_skipped_issues,
   465→        _steps_with_bad_paths,
   466→        _steps_with_vague_detail,
   467→        _steps_without_effort,
   468→    )
   469→
   470→    print(colorize("  Stage: ENRICH — Make steps executor-ready (detail, refs)", "bold"))
   471→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
   472→
   473→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   474→    if shallow:
   475→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   476→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot confirm: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   477→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   478→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   479→        print()
   480→        print(colorize("  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).", "dim"))
   481→        print(colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   482→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   483→        return
   484→    else:
   485→        print(colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   486→
   487→    # Block on bad file paths at confirm time
   488→    from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   489→    bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, get_project_root())
   490→    if bad_paths:
   491→        total_bad = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   492→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {total_bad} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk.", "red"))
   493→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths:
   494→            for p in paths:
   495→                print(colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {p}", "yellow"))
   496→        print(colorize("  Fix paths with: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail '...'", "dim"))
   497→        return
   498→
   499→    # Block on missing effort tags (was advisory, now blocking)
   500→    untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   501→    if untagged:
   502→        total_missing = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   503→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {total_missing} step(s) have no effort tag.", "red"))
   504→        for name, missing, total in untagged[:5]:
   505→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {missing}/{total} steps missing effort", "yellow"))
   506→        print(colorize("  Every step needs --effort (trivial/small/medium/large).", "dim"))
   507→        print(colorize("  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small", "dim"))
   508→        return
   509→
   510→    # Block on missing issue_refs (traceability)
   511→    no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   512→    if no_refs:
   513→        total_missing = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   514→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {total_missing} step(s) have no issue_refs.", "red"))
   515→        for name, missing, total in no_refs[:5]:
   516→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {missing}/{total} steps missing refs", "yellow"))
   517→        print(colorize("  Every step needs --issue-refs linking it to the review issue(s) it addresses.", "dim"))
   518→        print(colorize("  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --issue-refs <hash1> <hash2>", "dim"))
   519→        return
   520→
   521→    # Block on vague detail (too short, no file paths)
   522→    vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, get_project_root())
   523→    if vague:
   524→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths).", "red"))
   525→        for name, step_num, title in vague[:5]:
   526→            print(colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {title}", "yellow"))
   527→        print(colorize("  Executor-ready means: someone with zero context knows which file to open and what to change.", "dim"))
   528→        print(colorize("  Add file paths and specific instructions to each step's --detail.", "dim"))
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    # Warn on steps referencing skipped/wontfixed issues
   532→    stale_refs = _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan)
   533→    if stale_refs:
   534→        total_stale = sum(len(ids) for _, _, ids in stale_refs)
   535→        print(colorize(f"\n  Warning: {total_stale} step issue_ref(s) point to skipped/wontfixed issues.", "yellow"))
   536→        for name, step_num, ids in stale_refs[:5]:

   538→        print(colorize("  Consider removing stale refs or removing the step if it's no longer needed.", "dim"))
   539→
   540→    enrich_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   541→
   542→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   543→        if attestation:
   544→            print(colorize(
   545→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   546→                "red",
   547→            ))
   548→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   549→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich --attestation "Steps are executor-ready..."', "dim"))
   550→        return
   551→
   552→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   553→        attestation.strip(), "enrich", cluster_names=enrich_clusters,
   554→    )
   555→    if validation_err:
   556→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   557→        return
   558→
   559→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   560→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   561→    purge_triage_stage(plan, "enrich")
   562→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   563→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   564→        plan,
   565→        "triage_confirm_enrich",
   566→        actor="user",
   567→        detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()},
   568→    )
   569→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   570→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Enrich confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
   571→    print_user_message(
   572→        "Hey — enrich is confirmed. Run `desloppify plan triage"
   573→        " --stage sense-check --report \"...\"` to verify step"
   574→        " accuracy and cross-cluster dependencies."
   575→    )

   578→def _confirm_sense_check(
   579→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   580→    plan: dict,
   581→    stages: dict,
   582→    attestation: str | None,
   583→    *,
   584→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   585→) -> None:
   586→    """Show sense-check summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   587→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   588→    if "sense-check" not in stages:
   589→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   590→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   591→        return
   592→    if stages["sense-check"].get("confirmed_at"):
   593→        print(colorize("  Sense-check stage already confirmed.", "green"))
   594→        return
   595→
   596→    # Re-run all enrich-level validations
   597→    from ._stage_validation import (
   598→        _shallow_steps,
   599→        _steps_missing_issue_refs,
   600→        _steps_with_bad_paths,
   601→        _steps_with_vague_detail,
   602→        _steps_without_effort,
   603→    )
   604→    from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   605→
   606→    print(colorize("  Stage: SENSE-CHECK — Verify accuracy & cross-cluster deps", "bold"))
   607→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 57, "dim"))
   608→
   609→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   610→
   611→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   612→    if shallow:
   613→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   614→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot confirm: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   615→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   616→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   617→        return
   618→
   619→    bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   620→    if bad_paths:
   621→        total_bad = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   622→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {total_bad} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk.", "red"))
   623→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths:
   624→            for p in paths:
   625→                print(colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {p}", "yellow"))
   626→        return
   627→
   628→    untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   629→    if untagged:
   630→        total_missing = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   631→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {total_missing} step(s) have no effort tag.", "red"))
   632→        return
   633→
   634→    no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   635→    if no_refs:
   636→        total_missing = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   637→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {total_missing} step(s) have no issue_refs.", "red"))
   638→        return
   639→
   640→    vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   641→    if vague:
   642→        print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail.", "red"))
   643→        return
   644→
   645→    print(colorize("  All enrich-level checks pass.", "green"))
   646→
   647→    sense_check_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   648→
   649→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   650→        if attestation:
   651→            print(colorize(
   652→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   653→                "red",
   654→            ))
   655→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   656→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check --attestation "Content and structure verified..."', "dim"))
   657→        return
   658→
   659→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   660→        attestation.strip(), "sense-check", cluster_names=sense_check_clusters,
   661→    )
   662→    if validation_err:
   663→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   664→        return
   665→
   666→    stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   667→    stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   668→    purge_triage_stage(plan, "sense-check")
   669→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   670→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   671→        plan,
   672→        "triage_confirm_sense_check",
   673→        actor="user",
   674→        detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()},
   675→    )
   676→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   677→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Sense-check confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
   678→    print_user_message(
   679→        "Hey — sense-check is confirmed. Run `desloppify plan triage"
   680→        " --complete --strategy \"...\"` to finish triage."

   682→
   683→
   684→def _cmd_confirm_stage(
   685→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   686→    *,
   687→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   688→) -> None:
   689→    """Router for ``--confirm observe/reflect/organize/enrich/sense-check``."""
   690→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   691→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
   692→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   693→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   694→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   695→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   696→
   697→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
   698→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   699→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
   700→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   701→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
   702→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   703→    elif confirm_stage == "enrich":
   704→        _confirm_enrich(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   705→    elif confirm_stage == "sense-check":
   706→        _confirm_sense_check(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   707→
   708→
   709→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
   710→validate_attestation = _validate_attestation
   711→
   712→
   713→def cmd_confirm_stage(
   714→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   715→    *,
   716→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   717→) -> None:
   718→    """Public triage confirmation entrypoint."""
   719→    _cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=services)
   720→
   721→__all__ = [
   722→    "MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   723→    "cmd_confirm_stage",
   724→    "validate_attestation",
   725→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   726→    "_cmd_confirm_stage",
   727→    "_confirm_enrich",
   728→    "_confirm_observe",
   729→    "_confirm_organize",
   730→    "_confirm_reflect",
   731→    "_confirm_sense_check",
   732→    "_validate_attestation",
   733→]
   734→

1→"""Internal sync helpers for auto-cluster regeneration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_strategy import (
     9→    cluster_name_from_key as _cluster_name_from_key,
    10→    generate_action as _generate_action,
    11→    generate_description as _generate_description,
    12→    grouping_key as _grouping_key,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    15→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    16→    _current_stale_ids,
    17→    current_under_target_ids,
    18→    current_unscored_ids,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    21→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    22→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    34→
    35→
    36→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    37→    """Collect all issue IDs belonging to manual (non-auto) clusters."""
    38→    ids: set[str] = set()
    39→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    40→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    41→            ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    42→    return ids
    43→
    44→
    45→def _group_clusterable_issues(
    46→    issues: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
    49→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
    50→    """Group open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues by detector/subtype key.
    51→
    52→    Returns (groups_by_key, issue_data) where groups_by_key maps grouping keys
    53→    to lists of issue IDs, filtered to clusters >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE.
    54→    """
    55→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    56→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    57→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
    58→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    59→            continue
    60→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    61→            continue
    62→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    66→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    67→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
    68→        if key is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        groups[key].append(fid)
    72→        issue_data[fid] = issue
    73→
    74→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
    75→    return groups, issue_data
    76→
    77→
    78→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    clusters: dict,
    82→    existing_name: str,
    83→    member_ids: list[str],
    84→    now: str,
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Sync member IDs for a user-modified cluster, returns count of changes."""
    87→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    88→    changes = 0
    89→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
    91→    if new_ids:
    92→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
    93→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
    94→        changes = 1
    95→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    96→    for fid in member_ids:
    97→        if fid not in overrides:
    98→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    99→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   100→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   101→    return changes
   102→
   103→
   104→def _subjective_state_sets(
   105→    state: StateModel,
   106→    *,
   107→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   108→    target_strict: float,
   109→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
   110→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
   111→    if policy is not None:
   112→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
   113→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
   114→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
   115→    else:
   116→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   117→        stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   118→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   119→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
   120→
   121→
   122→def _has_objective_backlog(
   123→    issues: dict,
   124→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   125→) -> bool:
   126→    """Check if objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   127→    if policy is not None:
   128→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   129→    return any(
   130→        f.get("status") == "open"
   131→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   132→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   133→        for f in issues.values()
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   138→    plan: dict,
   139→    clusters: dict,
   140→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   141→    *,
   142→    cluster_key: str,
   143→    cluster_name: str,
   144→    member_ids: list[str],
   145→    description: str,
   146→    action: str,
   147→    now: str,
   148→    optional: bool = False,
   149→) -> int:
   150→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
   151→    changes = 0
   152→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   153→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   154→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   155→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   157→        if (
   158→            old_ids != new_ids_set
   159→            or cluster.get("description") != description
   160→            or cluster.get("action") != action
   161→        ):
   162→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   163→            cluster["description"] = description
   164→            cluster["action"] = action
   165→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   166→            changes = 1
   167→    else:
   168→        new_cluster = {
   169→            "name": cluster_name,
   170→            "description": description,
   171→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
   172→            "created_at": now,
   173→            "updated_at": now,
   174→            "auto": True,
   175→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   176→            "action": action,
   177→            "user_modified": False,
   178→        }
   179→        if optional:
   180→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   181→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   182→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   183→        changes = 1
   184→
   185→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   186→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   187→    for fid in member_ids:
   188→        if fid not in overrides:
   189→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   190→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   191→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   192→
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def sync_issue_clusters(
   197→    plan: dict,
   198→    issues: dict,
   199→    clusters: dict,
   200→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   201→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   202→    now: str,
   203→) -> int:
   204→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   205→    changes = 0
   206→
   207→    groups, issue_data = _group_clusterable_issues(
   208→        issues, manual_member_ids=_manual_member_ids(clusters)
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   212→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   213→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   214→
   215→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   216→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   217→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   218→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   219→
   220→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   221→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   222→
   223→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   224→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   225→
   226→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   227→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   228→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   229→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   230→                changes += _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   231→                    plan,
   232→                    clusters=clusters,
   233→                    existing_name=existing_name,
   234→                    member_ids=member_ids,
   235→                    now=now,
   236→                )
   237→                continue
   238→
   239→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   240→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   241→
   242→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   243→            plan,
   244→            clusters,
   245→            existing_by_key,
   246→            cluster_key=key,
   247→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   248→            member_ids=member_ids,
   249→            description=description,
   250→            action=action,
   251→            now=now,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    return changes
   255→
   256→
   257→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   258→    plan: dict,
   259→    state: StateModel,
   260→    issues: dict,
   261→    clusters: dict,
   262→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   263→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   264→    now: str,
   265→    *,
   266→    target_strict: float,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> int:
   270→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   271→    changes = 0
   272→
   273→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   274→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   275→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   279→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   280→    )
   281→
   282→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   283→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   284→    )
   285→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   286→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   287→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   288→    )
   289→
   290→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   291→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   292→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   293→        description = (
   294→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   295→        )
   296→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   297→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   298→            plan,
   299→            clusters,
   300→            existing_by_key,
   301→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   302→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   303→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   304→            description=description,
   305→            action=action,
   306→            now=now,
   307→        )
   308→
   309→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   310→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   311→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   312→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   313→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   314→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   315→            plan,
   316→            clusters,
   317→            existing_by_key,
   318→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   319→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   320→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   321→            description=description,
   322→            action=action,
   323→            now=now,
   324→        )
   325→
   326→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   327→
   328→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   329→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   330→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   331→    ut_prune = [
   332→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   333→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   334→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   335→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   336→        and fid in order
   337→    ]
   338→    for fid in ut_prune:
   339→        order.remove(fid)
   340→        changes += 1
   341→
   342→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   343→
   344→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   345→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   346→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   347→        description = (
   348→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   349→            f"dimensions under target score"
   350→        )
   351→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   352→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   353→            plan,
   354→            clusters,
   355→            existing_by_key,
   356→            [REDACTED],
   357→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   358→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   359→            description=description,
   360→            action=action,
   361→            now=now,
   362→            optional=True,
   363→        )
   364→
   365→        existing_order = set(order)
   366→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   367→            if fid not in existing_order:
   368→                order.append(fid)
   369→
   370→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   371→        objective_evict = [
   372→            fid for fid in order
   373→            if fid in under_target_ids
   374→        ]
   375→        for fid in objective_evict:
   376→            order.remove(fid)
   377→            changes += 1
   378→
   379→    return changes
   380→
   381→
   382→def prune_stale_clusters(
   383→    plan: dict,
   384→    issues: dict,
   385→    clusters: dict,
   386→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   387→    now: str,
   388→) -> int:
   389→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   390→    changes = 0
   391→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   392→        cluster = clusters[name]
   393→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   394→            continue
   395→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   396→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   397→            continue
   398→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   399→            alive = [
   400→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
```

1→"""Prompt template helpers for holistic review batch subagents."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (

    27→def _render_metadata_block(
    28→    *,
    29→    repo_root: Path,
    30→    packet_path: Path,
    31→    batch_index: int,
    32→    context: PromptBatchContext,
    33→) -> str:
    34→    return (
    35→        "You are a focused subagent reviewer for a single holistic investigation batch.\n\n"
    36→        f"Repository root: {repo_root}\n"
    37→        f"Blind packet: {packet_path}\n"
    38→        f"Batch index: {batch_index + 1}\n"
    39→        f"Batch name: {context.name}\n"
    40→        f"Batch rationale: {context.rationale}\n\n"
    41→    )
    42→
    43→
    44→def _render_output_schema(context: PromptBatchContext, batch_index: int) -> str:
    45→    return (
    46→        "Output schema:\n"
    47→        "{\n"
    48→        f'  "batch": "{context.name}",\n'
    49→        f'  "batch_index": {batch_index + 1},\n'
    50→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'
    51→        '  "dimension_notes": {\n'
    52→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
    53→        '      "evidence": ["specific code observations"],\n'
    54→        '      "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    55→        '      "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    56→        '      "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    57→        f'      "{DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY}": "required when score >{HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f}",\n'
    58→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
    59→        "    }\n"
    60→        "  },\n"
    61→        '  "dimension_judgment": {\n'
    62→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
    63→        '      "strengths": ["0-5 specific things the codebase does well from this dimension\'s perspective"],\n'
    64→        '      "issue_character": "one sentence characterizing the nature/pattern of issues from this dimension\'s perspective",\n'
    65→        '      "score_rationale": "2-3 sentences explaining the score from this dimension\'s perspective, referencing global anchors"\n'
    66→        "    }\n"
    67→        "  },\n"
    68→        '  "issues": [{\n'
    69→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
    70→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
    71→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
    72→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
    73→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
    74→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
    75→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
    76→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
    77→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
    78→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history"\n'
    79→        "  }],\n"
    80→        '  "retrospective": {\n'
    81→        '    "root_causes": ["optional: concise root-cause hypotheses"],\n'
    82→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
    83→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
    84→        "  }\n"
    85→        "}\n"
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _extract_dimension_prompts(batch: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    90→    """Extract dimension prompts embedded by explode_to_single_dimension."""
    91→    prompt = batch.get("_dimension_prompt")
    92→    if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
    93→        return {}
    94→    dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
    95→    if isinstance(dims, list) and len(dims) == 1:
    96→        return {str(dims[0]): prompt}
    97→    return {}
    98→
    99→
   100→def render_batch_prompt(
   101→    *,
   102→    repo_root: Path,
   103→    packet_path: Path,
   104→    batch_index: int,
   105→    batch: dict[str, object],
   106→) -> str:
   107→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   108→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
   109→    dim_prompts = _extract_dimension_prompts(batch)
   110→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   111→        _render_metadata_block(
   112→            repo_root=repo_root,
   113→            packet_path=packet_path,
   114→            batch_index=batch_index,
   115→            context=context,
   116→        ),
   117→        render_dimension_prompts_block(context.dimensions, dim_prompts),
   118→        render_scoring_frame(),
   119→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   120→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   121→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   122→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   123→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   124→        render_scope_enums(),
   125→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]

1→"""Shared prompt rendering sections used by both batch and external review paths."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
     8→    max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    13→class PromptBatchContext:
    14→    name: str
    15→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...]
    16→    rationale: str
    17→    seed_files: tuple[str, ...]
    18→    issues_cap: int
    19→
    20→    @property
    21→    def dimension_set(self) -> set[str]:
    22→        return set(self.dimensions)
    23→
    24→    @property
    25→    def dimensions_text(self) -> str:
    26→        return ", ".join(self.dimensions) if self.dimensions else "(none)"
    27→
    28→    @property
    29→    def seed_files_text(self) -> str:
    30→        return "\n".join(f"- {path}" for path in self.seed_files) if self.seed_files else "- (none)"
    31→
    32→
    33→def coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    34→    if not isinstance(raw, list | tuple):
    35→        return ()
    36→    return tuple(str(item) for item in raw if isinstance(item, str) and item)
    37→
    38→
    39→def build_batch_context(batch: dict[str, object], batch_index: int) -> PromptBatchContext:
    40→    dimensions = coerce_string_list(batch.get("dimensions", []))
    41→    return PromptBatchContext(
    42→        name=str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}")),
    43→        dimensions=dimensions,
    44→        rationale=str(batch.get("why", "")).strip(),
    45→        seed_files=coerce_string_list(batch.get("files_to_read", [])),
    46→        issues_cap=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dimensions)),
    47→    )
    48→
    49→
    50→SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION = {
    51→    "initialization_coupling": (
    52→        "9e. For initialization_coupling, use evidence from "
    53→        "`holistic_context.scan_evidence.mutable_globals` and "
    54→        "`holistic_context.errors.mutable_globals`. Investigate initialization ordering "
    55→        "dependencies, coupling through shared mutable state, and whether state should "
    56→        "be encapsulated behind a proper registry/context manager.\n"
    57→    ),
    58→    "design_coherence": (
    59→        "9f. For design_coherence, use evidence from "
    60→        "`holistic_context.scan_evidence.signal_density` — files where "
    61→        "multiple mechanical detectors fired. Investigate what design change would address "
    62→        "multiple signals simultaneously. Check `scan_evidence.complexity_hotspots` for "
    63→        "files with high responsibility cluster counts.\n"
    64→    ),
    65→    "error_consistency": (
    66→        "9g. For error_consistency, use evidence from "
    67→        "`holistic_context.errors.exception_hotspots` — files with "
    68→        "concentrated exception handling issues. Investigate whether error handling is "
    69→        "designed or accidental. Check for broad catches masking specific failure modes.\n"
    70→    ),
    71→    "cross_module_architecture": (
    72→        "9h. For cross_module_architecture, also consult "
    73→        "`holistic_context.coupling.boundary_violations` for import paths that "
    74→        "cross architectural boundaries, and `holistic_context.dependencies.deferred_import_density` "
    75→        "for files with many function-level imports (proxy for cycle pressure).\n"
    76→    ),
    77→    "convention_outlier": (
    78→        "9i. For convention_outlier, also consult "
    79→        "`holistic_context.conventions.duplicate_clusters` for cross-file "
    80→        "function duplication and `conventions.naming_drift` for directory-level naming "
    81→        "inconsistency.\n"
    82→    ),
    83→}
    84→
    85→
    86→def render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
    87→    """Render dimension-specific scan_evidence guidance."""
    88→    return "".join(
    89→        text
    90→        for dim, text in SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION.items()
    91→        if dim in dim_set
    92→    )
    93→
    94→
    95→def render_historical_focus(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    96→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
    97→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
    98→        return ""
    99→
   100→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   101→    try:
   102→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   103→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   104→        selected_count = 0
   105→
   106→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   107→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   108→        issues = []
   109→
   110→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   111→        return ""
   112→
   113→    lines: list[str] = []
   114→    lines.append(
   115→        "Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:"
   116→    )
   117→    lines.append(
   118→        "Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report"
   119→        " issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or"
   120→        " what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out."
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    for entry in issues:
   124→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   125→            continue
   126→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   127→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   128→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   129→
   130→        line = f"  - [{status}] {summary}"
   131→        if note:
   132→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   133→        lines.append(line)
   134→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   135→
   136→
   137→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: dict[str, object]) -> str:
   138→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   139→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   140→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   141→        return ""
   142→
   143→    lines: list[str] = []
   144→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:")
   145→    lines.append(
   146→        "Confirm or refute each with your own code reading. Report only confirmed defects."
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    shown = 0
   150→    for entry in signals:
   151→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   152→            continue
   153→        file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   154→        concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   155→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   156→        question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   157→        evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   158→        evidence = (
   159→            [str(item).strip() for item in evidence_raw if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
   160→            if isinstance(evidence_raw, list)
   161→            else []
   162→        )
   163→
   164→        lines.append(f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}")
   165→        if summary:
   166→            lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   167→        if question:
   168→            lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   169→        for snippet in evidence[:2]:
   170→            lines.append(f"    evidence: {snippet}")
   171→        shown += 1
   172→        if shown >= 8:
   173→            break
   174→
   175→    extra = max(0, len(signals) - shown)
   176→    if extra:
   177→        lines.append(f"  - (+{extra} more concern signals)")
   178→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   179→
   180→
   181→def render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   182→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   183→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   184→        {
   185→            "cross_module_architecture",
   186→            "high_level_elegance",
   187→            "mid_level_elegance",
   188→            "design_coherence",
   189→            "initialization_coupling",
   190→        }
   191→    ):
   192→        return ""
   193→    return (
   194→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   195→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   196→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   197→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   198→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   199→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   200→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   205→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   206→        return ""
   207→    return (
   208→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   209→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   210→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   211→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   212→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   213→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   214→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_issues` for directories "
   215→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→def render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   220→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   221→        return ""
   222→    return (
   223→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   224→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   225→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   226→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   227→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   228→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   229→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   230→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   231→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   232→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   233→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   234→    )
   235→
   236→
   237→def render_dimension_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   238→    return (
   239→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   240→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   241→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   242→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   243→    )
   244→
   245→
   246→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   247→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   248→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   249→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   250→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   251→
   252→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   253→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension.  When *dimension_prompts*
   254→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a ``_dimension_prompt`` key with the
   255→    prompt for its single dimension so that downstream renderers can use it
   256→    without extra parameter threading.
   257→    """
   258→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   259→    result: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   260→    for batch in batches:
   261→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   262→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   263→            result.append(batch)
   264→            continue
   265→        for dim in dims:
   266→            exploded: dict[str, object] = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   267→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   268→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   269→                exploded["_dimension_prompt"] = dim_prompt
   270→            result.append(exploded)
   271→    return result
   272→
   273→
   274→def render_dimension_prompts_block(
   275→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   276→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   277→) -> str:
   278→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   279→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   280→        return ""
   281→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   282→    for dim in dimensions:
   283→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   284→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   285→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   286→            continue
   287→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   288→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   289→        if description:
   290→            lines.append(description)
   291→
   292→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   293→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   294→            lines.append("Look for:")
   295→            for item in look_for:
   296→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   297→
   298→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   299→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   300→            lines.append("Skip:")
```
