    79→def _observe_instructions() -> str:
    80→    return """\
    81→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    82→
    83→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    84→
    85→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    86→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    87→- Describe code that was already refactored
    88→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    89→- Count props/returns/args wrong
    90→
    91→Your job is to catch these. An observe report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    92→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    93→
    94→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. That's the next stage (reflect).
    95→Just verify: is each issue real?
    96→
    97→**CRITICAL: You must cite specific issue IDs (hash prefixes like [abcd1234]) in your report.**
    98→The confirmation gate requires citing at least 10% of issues (or 5, whichever is smaller).
    99→
   100→**USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension
   101→group — to investigate concurrently. Each subagent MUST:
   102→- Open and read the actual source file for EVERY assigned issue
   103→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
   104→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
   105→- Report a clear verdict per issue: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
   106→
   107→Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
   108→- Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
   109→- Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
   110→- Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
   111→- Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
   112→- Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
   113→
   114→**What a GOOD observe report looks like:**
   115→- "[34580232] taskType is plain string — FALSE POSITIVE. Uses branded string union KnownTaskType
   116→  with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist."
   117→- "[b634fc71] useGenerationsPaneController returns 60+ values — GENUINE. Confirmed 65 properties
   118→  at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation."
   119→
   120→**What a LAZY observe report looks like (will be rejected):**
   121→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
   122→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
   123→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
   124→
   125→**Your report must include for EVERY issue:**
   126→1. The hash prefix
   127→2. Your verdict (genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering)
   128→3. The specific evidence (what you found when you read the code)
   129→
   130→When done, run:
   131→```
   132→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<your analysis with [hash] citations>"
   133→```
   134→"""
   135→
   136→
   137→def _reflect_instructions() -> str:
   138→    return """\
   139→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   140→
   141→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   142→
   143→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   144→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   145→
   146→### What you must do:
   147→
   148→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from observe verdicts)?
   149→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   150→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   151→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? (with per-issue justification — "low priority" is
   152→   not a justification; "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication" is)
   153→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   154→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   155→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   156→   another round of fixes.
   157→
   158→### Your report MUST include a concrete cluster blueprint
   159→
   160→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   161→```
   162→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks": issues X, Y, Z (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   163→Cluster "task-typing": issues A, B (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   164→Skip: issue W (false positive per observe), issue V (over-engineering — fix adds 50 lines for 3 lines saved)
   165→```
   166→
   167→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   168→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   169→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   170→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   171→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   172→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   173→
   174→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   175→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   176→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   177→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   178→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   179→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering).
   180→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   181→
   182→When done, run:
   183→```
   184→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<your strategy with cluster blueprint>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning dimensions or recurring patterns>"
   185→```
   186→"""
   187→
   188→
   189→def _organize_instructions() -> str:
   190→    return """\
   191→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   192→
   193→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   194→
   195→The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go
   196→where, what to skip. Build it using the CLI. If something doesn't work as planned
   197→(issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust and document why.
   198→
   199→This stage should be largely mechanical. If you find yourself making major strategic
   200→decisions, something went wrong in reflect — the strategy should already be decided.
   201→
   202→### Process
   203→
   204→1. Review the reflect report's cluster blueprint (provided below)
   205→2. **Skip false positives and over-engineering** identified in observe/reflect. Every skip needs a
   206→   per-issue justification — not "low priority" but "false positive: the code at line 47
   207→   already uses named constants, contradicting the issue's claim":
   208→   ```
   209→   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<specific per-issue reason>" --attest "<attestation>"
   210→   ```
   211→3. Create clusters as specified in the blueprint:
   212→   `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
   213→4. Add issues: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <patterns...>`
   214→5. Add steps that consolidate: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue
   215→6. Set `--effort` on each step individually (trivial/small/medium/large)
   216→7. Set `--depends-on` when clusters touch overlapping files
   217→
   218→### Quality gates (the confirmation will check these)
   219→
   220→Before recording, verify:
   221→- [ ] Every cluster name describes an area or specific change, not a problem type
   222→- [ ] No cluster has issues from 5+ unrelated directories (theme-group smell)
   223→- [ ] Step count < issue count (consolidation happened)
   224→- [ ] Every skip has a specific per-issue reason (not "low priority")
   225→- [ ] Overlapping clusters have --depends-on set
   226→- [ ] Cluster descriptions describe the WORK, not the PROBLEMS
   227→
   228→Every review issue must end up in a cluster OR be skipped.
   229→
   230→When done, run:
   231→```
   232→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary of priorities and organization>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   233→```
   234→"""
   235→
   236→
   237→def _enrich_instructions() -> str:
   238→    return """\
   239→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   240→
   241→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   242→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   243→
   244→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   245→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   246→
   247→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   248→
   249→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   250→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   251→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   252→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   253→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   254→
   255→### How to enrich
   256→
   257→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   258→
   259→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get current steps and issue list
   260→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   261→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   262→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   263→   function name), and the exact change to make
   264→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   265→
   266→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   267→
   268→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   269→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   270→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   271→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   272→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   273→
   274→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   275→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   276→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   277→
   278→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   279→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   280→
   281→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   282→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   283→
   284→### Examples
   285→
   286→**GOOD step detail:**
   287→```
   288→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   289→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   290→previous state and change showToast from false to true."
   291→--issue-refs 79baeebf --effort small
   292→```
   293→
   294→**BAD step detail (will be rejected):**
   295→```
   296→--detail "Fix silent error swallowing"  # No file, no location, no action
   297→--detail "Decompose god-hooks"  # What file? What hooks? Into what?
   298→--detail "Address the issues identified in the observe stage"  # This says nothing
   299→```
   300→
   301→### Do NOT mark steps as done
   302→
   303→Use `--update-step N` to add detail, effort, and issue-refs.
   304→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   305→
   306→### File path rules
   307→
   308→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create
   309→(e.g., a new shared module) or rename targets (the new filename after a rename). Reference
   310→the current source file and describe what will change. The path validator will block
   311→confirmation if paths don't exist on disk.
   312→
   313→When done, run:
   314→```
   315→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   316→```
   317→"""
   318→
   319→def _sense_check_instructions() -> str:
   320→    return """\
   321→## SENSE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   322→
   323→This stage is handled by two parallel subagents. If you are being run as a
   324→single-subprocess fallback, perform BOTH the content and structure checks below.
   325→
   326→### Content Check (per cluster)
   327→For EVERY step in every cluster, read the actual source file and verify:
   328→1. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match the step description?
   329→2. NAMES: Do function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?
   330→3. COUNTS: Are counts ("update the 3 imports") accurate?
   331→4. STALENESS: Is the problem still present, or already fixed?
   332→5. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step?
   333→6. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match actual scope?
   334→7. DUPLICATES: Flag steps that duplicate work in another cluster.
   335→8. OVER-ENGINEERING: Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:
   336→   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation
   337→   - Introduce unnecessary config/feature-flags/generalization
   338→   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit
   339→   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires
   340→   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)
   341→   Remove or simplify over-engineered steps. If the whole cluster is net-negative, say so.
   342→
   343→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..." --effort <tag>`
   344→
   345→### Structure Check (global)
   346→Build a file-touch graph and check:
   347→1. SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without --depends-on → add dependency
   348→2. MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates → add cascade step
   349→3. CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles, don't add them
   350→
   351→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other>`
   352→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "..." --detail "..." --effort trivial --issue-refs <hash>`
   353→
   354→When done, run:
   355→```
   356→desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<findings summary>"
   357→```
   358→"""
   359→
   360→
   361→_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS = {
   362→    "observe": _observe_instructions,
   363→    "reflect": _reflect_instructions,
   364→    "organize": _organize_instructions,
   365→    "enrich": _enrich_instructions,
   366→    "sense-check": _sense_check_instructions,
   367→}
   368→
   369→
   370→def build_stage_prompt(
   371→    stage: str,
   372→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   373→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   374→    *,
   375→    repo_root: Path,
   376→) -> str:
   377→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   378→    parts: list[str] = []
   379→
   380→    # Preamble
   381→    parts.append(_PREAMBLE.format(stage=stage.upper(), repo_root=repo_root))
   382→
   383→    # Issue data
   384→    issue_data = build_triage_prompt(triage_input)
   385→    parts.append("## Issue Data\n\n" + issue_data)
   386→
   387→    # Prior stage reports
   388→    if prior_reports:
   389→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   390→        for prior_stage, report in prior_reports.items():
   391→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   392→
   393→    # Stage-specific instructions
   394→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   395→    if instruction_fn:
   396→        parts.append(instruction_fn())
   397→
   398→    # CLI reference
   399→    parts.append(_CLI_REFERENCE)
   400→
   401→    # Validation requirements
   402→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   403→
   404→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   405→
   406→
   407→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   408→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   409→    *,
   410→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   411→) -> None:
   412→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   413→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   414→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   415→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   416→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   417→    state = runtime.state
   418→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   419→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   420→
   421→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   422→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   423→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   424→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   425→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   426→        if prior_stage == stage:
   427→            break
   428→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   429→        if report:
   430→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   431→
   432→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root)
   433→    print(prompt)
   434→
   435→
   436→def _validation_requirements(stage: str) -> str:
   437→    """What must be true for the stage to pass validation."""
   438→    if stage == "observe":
   439→        return (
   440→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   441→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   442→            "- Report must cite at least 10% of issue IDs (or 5, whichever is smaller)\n"
   443→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation mentioning dimension names\n"
   444→        )
   445→    if stage == "reflect":
   446→        return (
   447→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   448→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   449→            "- Report must mention recurring dimension names (if any exist)\n"
   450→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation\n"
   451→        )
   452→    if stage == "organize":
   453→        return (
   454→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   455→            "- At least one manual cluster with issues must exist\n"
   456→            "- All manual clusters must have description and action steps\n"
   457→            "- All review issues must be in a cluster or skipped\n"
   458→            "- Overlapping clusters must have --depends-on relationships\n"
   459→            "- Cluster descriptions must reflect current issues (not stale/skipped ones)\n"
   460→            "- Clusters must group by file/area proximity, not by dimension or theme\n"
   461→            "- A cluster whose issues span 5+ unrelated directories will be flagged\n"
   462→            "- Step count should be less than issue count (consolidate shared-file changes)\n"
   463→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"
   464→        )
   465→    if stage == "enrich":
   466→        return (
   467→            "## Validation Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — not advisory)\n"
   468→            "- Every step needs --detail with 80+ chars INCLUDING a file path\n"
   469→            "- Every step needs --issue-refs linking to review issue(s)\n"
   470→            "- Every step needs --effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)\n"
   471→            "- No bad file paths in step details (must exist on disk)\n"
   472→            "- No steps referencing skipped/wontfixed issues\n"
   473→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"
   474→        )
   475→    if stage == "sense-check":
   476→        return (
   477→            "## Validation Requirements (ALL BLOCKING)\n"
   478→            "- Re-runs ALL enrich-level checks (detail, issue_refs, effort, paths, vagueness)\n"
   479→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   480→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation mentioning cluster names\n"
   481→        )
   482→    return ""
   483→
   484→
   485→def _observe_batch_instructions(issue_count: int, total_batches: int) -> str:
   486→    return f"""\
   487→## OBSERVE Batch Instructions
   488→
   489→You are one of {total_batches} parallel observe batches. Your task: verify every issue
   490→assigned to you against the actual source code.
   491→
   492→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
   493→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
   494→- Describe code that was already refactored
   495→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
   496→- Count props/returns/args wrong
   497→
   498→Your job is to catch these. A report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
   499→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
   500→
   501→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. Just verify: is each issue real?
   502→
   503→**For EVERY issue you must:**
   504→- Open and read the actual source file
   505→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
   506→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
   507→- Report a clear verdict: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
   508→
   509→**What a GOOD report looks like:**
   510→- "[34580232] taskType is plain string — FALSE POSITIVE. Uses branded string union KnownTaskType
   511→  with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist."
   512→- "[b634fc71] useGenerationsPaneController returns 60+ values — GENUINE. Confirmed 65 properties
   513→  at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation."
   514→
   515→**What a LAZY report looks like (will be rejected):**
   516→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
   517→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
   518→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
   519→
   520→**Your report must include for EVERY issue ({issue_count} total):**
   521→1. The hash prefix in brackets
   522→2. Your verdict (genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering)
   523→3. The specific evidence (what you found when you read the code)
   524→
   525→## IMPORTANT: Output Rules
   526→
   527→**Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands.** Do NOT run `desloppify plan triage --stage observe`.
   528→You are a parallel batch — the orchestrator will merge all batch outputs and record the stage.
   529→
   530→**Write your analysis as plain text only.** Format:
   531→```
   532→[hash_prefix] VERDICT — evidence
   533→```
   534→"""
   535→
   536→
   537→def build_observe_batch_prompt(
   538→    batch_index: int,
   539→    total_batches: int,
   540→    dimension_group: list[str],
   541→    issues_subset: dict[str, dict],
   542→    *,
   543→    repo_root: Path,
   544→) -> str:
   545→    """Build a scoped observe prompt for a single dimension-group batch.
   546→
   547→    Unlike build_stage_prompt(), this produces a prompt for observe only,
   548→    scoped to a subset of issues. The batch subprocess writes analysis to
   549→    stdout — it does NOT run ``desloppify plan triage --stage observe``.
   550→    The orchestrator merges batch outputs and records observe once.
   551→    """
   552→    parts: list[str] = []
   553→
   554→    # Batch context header
   555→    parts.append(
   556→        f"You are observe batch {batch_index}/{total_batches}.\n"
   557→        f"Dimensions assigned to you: {', '.join(dimension_group)}\n"
   558→        f"Total issues in this batch: {len(issues_subset)}\n\n"
   559→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   560→    )
   561→
   562→    # Issue data — inline the subset directly
   563→    parts.append("## Issues to Verify\n")
   564→    for fid, f in sorted(issues_subset.items()):
   565→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   566→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   567→        title = f.get("title", fid)
   568→        file_path = detail.get("file_path", "")
   569→        description = detail.get("description", f.get("description", ""))
   570→        line = f"- [{fid[:8]}] ({dim}) **{title}**"
   571→        if file_path:
   572→            line += f" — `{file_path}`"
   573→        if description:
   574→            line += f"\n  {description[:300]}"
   575→        parts.append(line)
   576→
   577→    # Batch-specific observe instructions (no subagent/CLI references)
   578→    parts.append(_observe_batch_instructions(len(issues_subset), total_batches))
   579→
   580→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   581→
   582→
   583→def build_sense_check_content_prompt(
   584→    *,
   585→    cluster_name: str,
   586→    plan: dict,
   587→    repo_root: Path,
   588→) -> str:
   589→    """Build a content-verification prompt for a single cluster."""
   590→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   591→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   592→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   593→
   594→    parts: list[str] = []
   595→    parts.append(
   596→        f"You are sense-checking cluster `{cluster_name}` "
   597→        f"({len(steps)} steps, {len(issue_ids)} issues).\n"
   598→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   599→    )
   600→
```

    23→def validate_stage(

    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})

    35→    if stage == "observe":
    36→        if "observe" not in stages:
    37→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
    38→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
    39→        if len(report) < 100:
    40→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    41→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
    42→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
    43→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
    44→        if issue_count > 0:
    45→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
    46→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
    47→                return False, (
    48→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
    49→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "

    53→
    54→    if stage == "reflect":
    55→        if "reflect" not in stages:
    56→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
    57→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
    58→        if len(report) < 100:
    59→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    60→        return True, ""
    61→

    63→        if "organize" not in stages:

    66→        if not manual:

    68→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)

    79→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
    80→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
    81→        if scattered:

    96→        if warnings:

   138→        shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)

   141→            return False, f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs after sense-check."
   142→        bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   143→        if bad_paths:
   144→            total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   145→            return False, f"{total} file path(s) don't exist on disk after sense-check."
   146→        untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)

   150→        no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   151→        if no_refs:

   154→        vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)

   161→
   162→def validate_completion(
   163→    plan: dict,
   164→    state: dict,
   165→    repo_root: Path,
   166→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   167→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   168→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   169→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   170→
   171→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"):
   172→        if required not in stages:
   173→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   174→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   175→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   176→
   177→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   178→    if not manual:
   179→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   180→
   181→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   182→    if gaps:
   183→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   184→
   185→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   186→    if unclustered:
   187→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   188→
   189→    # Quality warnings (non-blocking but logged)
   190→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   191→    # Check dependency ordering
   192→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   193→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   194→        if name in deps:
   195→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   196→
   197→    # Check for all-trivial clusters
   198→    all_trivial_clusters = []
   199→    total_steps = 0
   200→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   201→        if c.get("auto") or not c.get("issue_ids"):
   202→            continue
   203→        steps = c.get("action_steps") or []
   204→        total_steps += len(steps)
   205→        if steps and all(
   206→            isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") == "trivial" for s in steps
   207→        ):
   208→            all_trivial_clusters.append(name)
   209→
   210→    return True, ""
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_auto_attestation(
   214→    stage: str,
   215→    plan: dict,
   216→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   217→) -> str:
   218→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   219→    if stage == "observe":
   220→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   221→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   222→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   223→        return (
   224→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   225→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   226→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    if stage == "reflect":
   230→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   231→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   232→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   233→        return (
   234→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   235→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   236→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   237→        )

Perfect! I have found the key locations. Let me create a comprehensive summary of my findings:

## Summary

I've thoroughly searched the desloppify codebase and found the locations where task descriptions for agent review work are defined. Here are the key findings:

### 1. **Auto-Cluster Sync Task Descriptions** (Primary Location)
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py`

- **Line 293-296**: Task for unscored subjective dimensions:
  - Description: `"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"`
  - Action: `"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"`

- **Line 312-313**: Task for stale subjective dimensions:
  - Description: `"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"`
  - Action: `"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)`

- **Line 346-350**: Task for under-target dimensions:
  - Description: `"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} dimensions under target score"`
  - Action: `"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)`

### 2. **Review Batch Prompt Task Instructions**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py`

- **Line 309-313** in `render_scoring_frame()`: 
  ```
  "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimension. Judge how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective..."
  ```
  This is the core task instruction for subagent reviewers.

- **Line 337**: Task requirement line:
  ```
  "3. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's dimension."
  ```

### 3. **Triage Runner Instructions with "theme" Language**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts.py`

- **Line 100-112**: Observable subagent instructions that say:
  ```
  **USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension group — to investigate concurrently.
  
  Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
  - Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
  - Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
  - Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
  - Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
  - Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
  ```

### 4. **Validation Language with "Theme" Reference**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_validation.py`

- **Line 225**: Auto-attestation text mentioning "identifying themes":
  ```python
  f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
  f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
  f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
  ```

- **Line 83**: Advisory warning message:
  ```python
  "Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}"
  ```

### Key Finding
The batch system is correctly designed to create **ONE SUBAGENT PER DIMENSION** (not multiple dimensions per batch, not theme-based batching). The language in:
- `render_scoring_frame()` correctly says "this batch's dimension" (singular)
- `explode_to_single_dimension()` ensures each batch has exactly one dimension
- The auto_cluster_sync creates tasks for reviewing dimensions individually

The only concerning language is in the **auto-attestation text** (line 225 of stage_validation.py) which mentions "identifying themes" — this appears to be generic language for describing what the observe stage does (analyzing dimensions to find patterns/themes) but could be misinterpreted as suggesting theme-based batching instead of dimension-based.

    23→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    24→    if len(text) <= width:
    25→        return text
    26→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _print_queue_header(
    30→    *,
    31→    items: list[dict],
    32→    include_skipped: bool,
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    new_count: int = 0,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    total = len(items)
    37→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    38→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    39→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    42→    subjective = sum(
    43→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    44→    )
    45→
    46→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    47→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    48→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    49→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    50→
    51→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    52→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    53→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    54→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    55→        subjective=subjective,
    56→    )
    57→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    58→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    60→
    61→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    62→    if focus:
    63→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    64→
    65→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    66→        return
    67→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    68→        return
    69→    print(
    70→        colorize(
    71→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    72→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    73→            "dim",
    74→        )
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    79→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    80→        return items[:top]
    81→    return items

    84→_CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS = {
    85→    "auto/initial-review": "Initial subjective review",
    86→    "auto/stale-review": "Stale subjective review",
    87→    "auto/under-target-review": "Optional re-review",

    89→
    90→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    91→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",

    94→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    95→}
    96→

    98→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:
    99→    if cluster_name in _CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS:
   100→        return _CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS[cluster_name]
   101→    return _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   102→
   103→
   104→def _render_cluster_banner(item: dict, position: int, new_ids: set[str]) -> None:
   105→    """Render a collapsed cluster as a visually distinct banner."""
   106→    name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   107→    member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   108→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   109→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(name, action_type)
   110→    cluster_type_tag = "[auto]" if item.get("cluster_auto", True) else "[triage]"
   111→    new_in_cluster = sum(
   112→        1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   113→        if m.get("id") in new_ids
   114→    )
   115→    new_tag = f"  (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   116→    # Step progress badge
   117→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   118→    step_badge = ""
   119→    if action_steps:
   120→        done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   121→        step_badge = f"  [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps]"
   122→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   123→    command = item.get("primary_command", "")
   124→
   125→    label = f"{position}. {cluster_type_tag} {type_label} — {member_count} items{step_badge}{new_tag}"
   126→    width = max(len(label) + 4, 50)
   127→    bar = "─" * width
   128→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "dim"))
   129→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "bold"))
   130→    print(colorize(f"     Cluster: {name}", "dim"))
   131→    if summary:
   132→        print(colorize(f"     {summary}", "dim"))
   133→    if command:
   134→        print(colorize(f"     Run:      {command}", "cyan"))
   135→    print(colorize(f"     Drill in: desloppify plan queue --cluster {name}", "dim"))
   136→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "dim"))
   137→
   138→
   139→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
   140→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   141→    _new = new_ids or set()
   142→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   143→        pos = str(idx)
   144→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   145→
   146→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   147→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   148→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   149→            conf_str = "—"
   150→            detector = "planning"
   151→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   152→            cluster_name = ""
   153→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   154→            conf_str = "—"
   155→            detector = "workflow"
   156→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   157→            cluster_name = ""
   158→        elif kind == "cluster":
   159→            # Clusters are rendered as banners, not table rows
   160→            continue
   161→        else:
   162→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   163→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   164→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   165→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   166→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   167→
   168→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   169→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   170→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   171→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   172→    return rows
   173→
   174→
   175→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   176→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   177→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   178→    state = runtime.state
   179→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   180→        return
   181→
   182→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   183→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   184→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   185→
   186→    plan = load_plan()
   187→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   188→
   189→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   190→    if not cluster_filter:
   191→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   192→        if active_cluster:
   193→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   194→
   195→    queue = build_work_queue(
   196→        state,
   197→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   198→            count=None,
   199→            status="open",
   200→            include_subjective=True,
   201→            plan=plan,
   202→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   203→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   204→        ),
   205→    )
   206→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   207→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   208→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   209→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   210→
   211→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   212→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   213→    # Merge review-based new issue IDs (since last triage)
   214→    review_new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   215→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   216→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   217→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   218→
   219→    if sort_by == "recent":
   220→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   221→
   222→    _print_queue_header(
   223→        items=items,
   224→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   225→        plan=plan,
   226→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    if not items:
   230→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    # Determine which items to show
   234→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   235→
   236→    # Render cluster banners first, then remaining items in the table
   237→    print()
   238→    cluster_count = 0
   239→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   240→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   241→            _render_cluster_banner(item, idx, new_ids)
   242→            cluster_count += 1
   243→
   244→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   245→    if rows:
   246→        headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   247→        widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   248→        print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   249→
   250→    if cluster_count and rows:
   251→        print(colorize(
   252→            f"  ({cluster_count} cluster banner{'s' if cluster_count != 1 else ''}"
   253→            " shown above — table shows remaining individual items)",
   254→            "dim",
   255→        ))
   256→
   257→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   258→        remaining = len(items) - top
   259→        print(colorize(
   260→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   261→        ))
   262→    print()
   263→
   264→
   265→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   266→

   311→def _render_item(
   312→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, issues_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   313→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   314→    single_item: bool = False,
   315→    header_showed_plan: bool = False,

   320→        kind_renderer(item)
   321→        return

   324→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))

   327→    _render_item_type(item)
   328→
   329→    if item.get("kind", "issue") == "subjective_dimension":
   330→        _render_subjective_dimension(item, explain=explain)
   331→        return
   332→
   333→    detail = _render_issue_detail(
   334→        item, single_item=single_item, header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   335→    )
   336→    _render_score_impact(item, dim_scores, potentials)

   338→    if explain:

   340→
   341→
   342→def _item_label(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> str:
   343→    queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   344→    if queue_pos and total > 1:
   345→        return f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   346→    if total > 1:
   347→        return f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}]"
   348→    pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   349→    return f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   350→
   351→
   352→def render_terminal_items(
   353→    items: list[dict],
   354→    dim_scores: dict,
   355→    issues_scoped: dict,
   356→    *,
   357→    group: str,
   358→    explain: bool,
   359→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   360→    plan: dict | None = None,
   361→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   362→) -> None:
   363→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster or cluster filter
   364→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   365→    header_showed_plan = False
   366→    if effective_cluster and plan:
   367→        cluster_name = effective_cluster
   368→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   369→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   370→        total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   371→        desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   372→        print(colorize(f"\n  ┌─ Cluster: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining) ─┐", "cyan"))
   373→        if desc:
   374→            print(colorize(f"  │ {desc}", "cyan"))
   375→        steps = cluster_data.get("action_steps") or []
   376→        if steps:
   377→            print(colorize("  │", "cyan"))
   378→            print(colorize("  │ Action plan:", "cyan"))
   379→            for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   380→                print(colorize(f"  │   {i}. {_step_text(step)}", "cyan"))
   381→        print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 60 + "┘", "cyan"))
   382→        print(colorize("  Back to full queue: desloppify next", "dim"))
   383→        if steps:
   384→            print(colorize(f"  Mark step done: desloppify plan cluster update {cluster_name} --done-step N", "dim"))
   385→        header_showed_plan = True
   386→
   387→    if group != "item":
   388→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   389→        return
   390→
   391→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   392→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   393→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   397→        if idx > 0:
   398→            print()
   399→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   400→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   401→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   402→            continue
   403→        label = _item_label(item, idx, len(items))
   404→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   405→        _render_item(
   406→            item, dim_scores, issues_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials,
   407→            single_item=len(items) == 1,
   408→            header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   409→        )
   410→
   411→
   412→__all__ = [
   413→    "render_terminal_items",
   414→]
   415→

1→"""Internal sync helpers for auto-cluster regeneration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_strategy import (

    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    34→
    35→
    36→def _manual_member_ids(clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    37→    """Collect all issue IDs belonging to manual (non-auto) clusters."""
    38→    ids: set[str] = set()
    39→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    40→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    41→            ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    42→    return ids
    43→
    44→
    45→def _group_clusterable_issues(
    46→    issues: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    manual_member_ids: set[str],
    49→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, dict]]:
    50→    """Group open, non-suppressed, non-manual issues by detector/subtype key.
    51→
    52→    Returns (groups_by_key, issue_data) where groups_by_key maps grouping keys
    53→    to lists of issue IDs, filtered to clusters >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE.
    54→    """
    55→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    56→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
    57→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
    58→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    59→            continue
    60→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    61→            continue
    62→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    66→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    67→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
    68→        if key is None:
    69→            continue
    70→
    71→        groups[key].append(fid)
    72→        issue_data[fid] = issue
    73→
    74→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
    75→    return groups, issue_data
    76→
    77→
    78→def _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
    79→    plan: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    clusters: dict,
    82→    existing_name: str,
    83→    member_ids: list[str],
    84→    now: str,
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Sync member IDs for a user-modified cluster, returns count of changes."""
    87→    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    88→    changes = 0
    89→    existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
    91→    if new_ids:
    92→        cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
    93→        cluster["updated_at"] = now
    94→        changes = 1
    95→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    96→    for fid in member_ids:
    97→        if fid not in overrides:
    98→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    99→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   100→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   101→    return changes
   102→
   103→
   104→def _subjective_state_sets(
   105→    state: StateModel,
   106→    *,
   107→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   108→    target_strict: float,
   109→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
   110→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
   111→    if policy is not None:
   112→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
   113→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
   114→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
   115→    else:
   116→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   117→        stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   118→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   119→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
   120→
   121→
   122→def _has_objective_backlog(
   123→    issues: dict,
   124→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   125→) -> bool:
   126→    """Check if objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   127→    if policy is not None:
   128→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   129→    return any(
   130→        f.get("status") == "open"
   131→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   132→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   133→        for f in issues.values()
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   138→    plan: dict,
   139→    clusters: dict,
   140→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   141→    *,
   142→    cluster_key: str,
   143→    cluster_name: str,
   144→    member_ids: list[str],
   145→    description: str,
   146→    action: str,
   147→    now: str,
   148→    optional: bool = False,
   149→) -> int:
   150→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
   151→    changes = 0
   152→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   153→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   154→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   155→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   157→        if (
   158→            old_ids != new_ids_set
   159→            or cluster.get("description") != description
   160→            or cluster.get("action") != action
   161→        ):
   162→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   163→            cluster["description"] = description
   164→            cluster["action"] = action
   165→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   166→            changes = 1
   167→    else:
   168→        new_cluster = {
   169→            "name": cluster_name,
   170→            "description": description,
   171→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
   172→            "created_at": now,
   173→            "updated_at": now,
   174→            "auto": True,
   175→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   176→            "action": action,
   177→            "user_modified": False,
   178→        }
   179→        if optional:
   180→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   181→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   182→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   183→        changes = 1
   184→
   185→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   186→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   187→    for fid in member_ids:
   188→        if fid not in overrides:
   189→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   190→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   191→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   192→
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def sync_issue_clusters(
   197→    plan: dict,
   198→    issues: dict,
   199→    clusters: dict,
   200→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   201→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   202→    now: str,
   203→) -> int:
   204→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   205→    changes = 0
   206→
   207→    groups, issue_data = _group_clusterable_issues(
   208→        issues, manual_member_ids=_manual_member_ids(clusters)
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   212→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   213→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   214→
   215→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   216→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   217→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   218→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   219→
   220→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   221→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   222→
   223→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   224→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   225→
   226→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   227→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   228→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   229→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   230→                changes += _sync_user_modified_cluster_members(
   231→                    plan,
   232→                    clusters=clusters,
   233→                    existing_name=existing_name,
   234→                    member_ids=member_ids,
   235→                    now=now,
   236→                )
   237→                continue
   238→
   239→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   240→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   241→
   242→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   243→            plan,
   244→            clusters,
   245→            existing_by_key,
   246→            cluster_key=key,
   247→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   248→            member_ids=member_ids,
   249→            description=description,
   250→            action=action,
   251→            now=now,
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    return changes
   255→
   256→
   257→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   258→    plan: dict,
   259→    state: StateModel,
   260→    issues: dict,
   261→    clusters: dict,
   262→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   263→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   264→    now: str,
   265→    *,
   266→    target_strict: float,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> int:
   270→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   271→    changes = 0
   272→
   273→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   274→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   275→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   279→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   280→    )
   281→
   282→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   283→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   284→    )
   285→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   286→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   287→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   288→    )
   289→
   290→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   291→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   292→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   293→        description = (
   294→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   295→        )
   296→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   297→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   298→            plan,
   299→            clusters,
   300→            existing_by_key,
   301→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   302→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   303→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   304→            description=description,
   305→            action=action,
   306→            now=now,
   307→        )
   308→
   309→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   310→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   311→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   312→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   313→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   314→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   315→            plan,
   316→            clusters,
   317→            existing_by_key,
   318→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   319→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   320→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   321→            description=description,
   322→            action=action,
   323→            now=now,
   324→        )
   325→
   326→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   327→
   328→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   329→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   330→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   331→    ut_prune = [
   332→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   333→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   334→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   335→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   336→        and fid in order
   337→    ]
   338→    for fid in ut_prune:
   339→        order.remove(fid)
   340→        changes += 1
   341→
   342→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   343→
   344→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   345→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   346→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   347→        description = (
   348→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   349→            f"dimensions under target score"
   350→        )
   351→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   352→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   353→            plan,
   354→            clusters,
   355→            existing_by_key,
   356→            [REDACTED],
   357→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   358→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   359→            description=description,
   360→            action=action,
   361→            now=now,
   362→            optional=True,
   363→        )
   364→
   365→        existing_order = set(order)
   366→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   367→            if fid not in existing_order:
   368→                order.append(fid)
   369→
   370→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   371→        objective_evict = [
   372→            fid for fid in order
   373→            if fid in under_target_ids
   374→        ]
   375→        for fid in objective_evict:
   376→            order.remove(fid)
   377→            changes += 1
   378→
   379→    return changes
   380→
   381→
   382→def prune_stale_clusters(
   383→    plan: dict,
   384→    issues: dict,
   385→    clusters: dict,
   386→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   387→    now: str,
   388→) -> int:
   389→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   390→    changes = 0
   391→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   392→        cluster = clusters[name]
   393→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   394→            continue
   395→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   396→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   397→            continue
   398→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   399→            alive = [
   400→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   401→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   402→            ]
   403→            if alive:
   404→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])):
   405→                    cluster["issue_ids"] = alive
   406→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   407→                    changes += 1
   408→                continue
   409→        del clusters[name]
   410→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   411→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   412→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   413→                override["cluster"] = None
   414→                override["updated_at"] = now
   415→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   416→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   417→        changes += 1
   418→    return changes
   419→
   420→
   421→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   422→

1→"""Pure grouping/description/action strategy for plan auto-clustering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DetectorMeta
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import AUTO_PREFIX
     9→
    10→
    11→def extract_subtype(issue: dict) -> str | None:
    12→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a issue."""
    13→    detail = issue.get("detail") or {}
    14→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    15→    if kind:
    16→        return kind
    17→
    18→    issue_id = issue.get("id", "")
    19→    parts = issue_id.split("::")
    20→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    21→        candidate = parts[-1]
    22→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    23→            return candidate
    24→    return None
    25→
    26→
    27→def grouping_key(issue: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    28→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a issue."""
    29→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    30→
    31→    if meta is None:
    32→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    33→
    34→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    35→        detail = issue.get("detail") or {}
    36→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    37→        if dimension:
    38→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    39→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    40→
    41→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in ("structural", "responsibility_cohesion"):
    42→        file_path = issue.get("file", "")
    43→        if file_path:
    44→            basename = os.path.basename(file_path)
    45→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    46→
    47→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    48→        subtype = extract_subtype(issue)
    49→        if subtype:
    50→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    51→
    52→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    53→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    54→
    55→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    56→
    57→
    58→def cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    59→    """Convert a grouping key to an ``auto/...`` cluster name."""
    60→    parts = key.split("::")
    61→    if len(parts) == 2:
    62→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    63→        if prefix_type == "review":
    64→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
    65→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
    66→    if len(parts) == 3:
    67→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
    68→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
    69→
    70→
    71→def generate_description(
    72→    cluster_name: str,
    73→    members: list[dict],
    74→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
    75→    subtype: str | None,
    76→) -> str:
    77→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
    78→    _ = cluster_name
    79→    count = len(members)
    80→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
    81→
    82→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    83→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
    84→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
    85→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review issues"
    86→
    87→    if detector == "structural":
    88→        files = {os.path.basename(member.get("file", "")) for member in members}
    89→        if len(files) == 1:
    90→            return f"Review file size: {next(iter(files))}"
    91→        return f"Review {count} large files"
    92→
    93→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
    94→    if subtype:
    95→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
    96→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
    97→
    98→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    99→        return f"Remove {count} {display} issues"
   100→
   101→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   102→
   103→
   104→def subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   105→    """Check if a subtype maps to a detector fixer."""
   106→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   107→        return None
   108→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   109→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   110→        return fixer_name
   111→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   112→        if subtype in fixer:
   113→            return fixer
   114→    return None
   115→
   116→
   117→def strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   118→    """Strip subtype examples from guidance text."""
   119→    if " — " in guidance:
   120→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   121→    return guidance
   122→
   123→
   124→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   125→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   126→    "refactor": "review and refactor each issue",
   127→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each issue",
   128→}
   129→
   130→
   131→def generate_action(
   132→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   133→    subtype: str | None,
   134→) -> str:
   135→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata."""
   136→    if meta is None:
   137→        return "review and fix each issue"
   138→
   139→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   140→        matched_fixer = subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   141→        if matched_fixer:
   142→            return f"desloppify autofix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   143→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   144→        return f"desloppify autofix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   145→
   146→    if meta.tool == "move":
   147→        return "desloppify move"
   148→
   149→    if meta.guidance:
   150→        if subtype:
   151→            return strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   152→        return meta.guidance
   153→
   154→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(meta.action_type, "review and fix each issue")
   155→
   156→
   157→__all__ = [
   158→    "cluster_name_from_key",
   159→    "generate_action",
   160→    "generate_description",
   161→    "grouping_key",
   162→    "strip_guidance_examples",
   163→]
   164→

    19→def do_prepare(

    40→
    41→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
    42→
    43→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    44→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    45→        state,
    46→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    47→    )
    48→    data = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
    49→        path,
    50→        lang_run,
    51→        state,
    52→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    53→            dimensions=dimensions,
    54→            files=found_files or None,
    55→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    56→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
    57→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    58→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    59→        ),
    60→    )
    61→    next_command = (
    62→        "desloppify review --prepare"
    63→    )
    64→    if retrospective:
    65→        next_command += (
    66→            " --retrospective"
    67→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
    68→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
    69→        )
    70→    data["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
    71→    data["narrative"] = narrative
    72→    data["next_command"] = next_command
    73→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    74→    if total == 0:
    75→        msg = f"no files found at path '{path}'. Nothing to review."
    76→        scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if isinstance(state, dict) else None
    77→        if scan_path:
    78→            msg += (
    79→                f"\nHint: your last scan used --path {scan_path}. "
    80→                f"Try: desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
    81→            )
    82→        else:
    83→            msg += "\nHint: pass --path <dir> matching the path used during scan."
    84→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
    85→    write_query(data)
    86→    _print_prepare_summary(data, next_command=next_command, retrospective=retrospective)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _print_prepare_summary(
    90→    data: dict, *, next_command: str, retrospective: bool,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Print the prepare summary to the terminal."""
    93→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    94→    batches = data.get("investigation_batches", [])
    95→    print(colorize(f"\n  Holistic review prepared: {total} files in codebase", "bold"))
    96→    if retrospective:
    97→        print(
    98→            colorize(
    99→                "  Retrospective context enabled: historical review issues injected into packet.",
   100→                "dim",
   101→            )
   102→        )
   103→    if batches:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                "\n  Investigation batches (independent — can run in parallel):", "bold"
   107→            )
   108→        )
   109→        for i, batch in enumerate(batches, 1):
   110→            n_files = len(batch["files_to_read"])
   111→            print(
   112→                colorize(
   113→                    f"    {i}. {batch['name']} ({n_files} files) — {batch['why']}",
   114→                    "dim",
   115→                )
   116→            )
   117→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "bold"))
   118→    for step_i, step in enumerate(data.get("workflow", []), 1):
   119→        print(colorize(f"    {step_i}. {step}", "dim"))
   120→    n_batches = len(data.get("investigation_batches", []))
   121→    print(colorize("\n  AGENT PLAN — pick the path matching your runner:", "yellow"))
   122→    print(
   123→        colorize(
   124→            "  1. Codex: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`",
   125→            "dim",
   126→        )
   127→    )
   128→    print(
   129→        colorize(
   130→            f"  2. Claude / other agent: `desloppify review --run-batches --dry-run`"
   131→            f" → generates {n_batches} prompt files in .desloppify/subagent_runs/<run>/prompts/."
   132→            f" Launch {n_batches} subagents in parallel (one per prompt),"
   133→            " write output to the matching results/ file,"
   134→            " then `desloppify review --import-run <run-dir> --scan-after-import`",
   135→            "dim",
   136→        )
   137→    )
   138→    print(
   139→        colorize(
   140→            "  3. Cloud/external: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` → follow template → `--external-submit`",
   141→            "dim",
   142→        )
   143→    )
   144→    print(
   145→        colorize(
   146→            "  4. Issues-only fallback: `desloppify review --import issues.json`",
   147→            "dim",
   148→        )
   149→    )
   150→    print(
   151→        colorize(
   152→            "  5. Emergency only: `--manual-override --attest \"<why>\"` (provisional; expires on next scan)",
   153→            "dim",
   154→        )
   155→    )
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            "\n  → query.json updated. Batches are pre-defined — do NOT regroup dimensions yourself.",
   159→            "cyan",
   160→        )
   161→    )
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = ["do_prepare"]
   165→
