```
   286→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   287→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   288→
   289→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   290→    """
   291→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   292→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   293→
   294→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   295→    if not summary_path.exists():
   296→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   297→    try:
   298→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   299→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   300→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   301→
   302→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   303→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   304→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   305→
   306→    if not successful:
   307→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   308→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   309→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   310→
   311→    try:
   312→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   313→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   314→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   315→
   316→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   317→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import_run(
   321→    run_dir_path: str,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file: str,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   329→    scan_path: str = ".",
   330→    dry_run: bool = False,
   331→) -> None:
   332→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   333→
   334→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   335→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   336→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   337→    """
   338→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   339→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   340→
   341→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   342→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   343→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   344→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   345→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   346→
   347→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   348→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   349→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   350→    output_files = {
   351→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   352→        for idx in selected_indexes
   353→    }
   354→
   355→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   356→    if missing:
   357→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   358→
   359→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   360→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   361→        failures=[],
   362→        output_files=output_files,
   363→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   364→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   365→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   366→            payload,
   367→            dims,
   368→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   369→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   370→        ),
   371→    )
   372→
   373→    if not batch_results:
   374→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   375→
   376→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   377→    if failures:
   378→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   379→
   380→    # -- merge --
   381→    merged = _merge_batch_results(batch_results)
   382→
   383→    # -- build provenance --
   384→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   385→    merged["provenance"] = build_batch_import_provenance(
   386→        runner=runner,
   387→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   388→        run_stamp=stamp,
   389→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   390→    )
   391→
   392→    # -- write merged output (always — this is inside the run dir, not state) --
   393→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   394→    safe_write_text(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   395→    print(colorize(f"  Merged output: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   396→
   397→    # -- import with trusted source --
   398→    _do_import(
   399→        str(merged_path),
```

```
    51→class ImportFlagValidationError(ValueError):

    67→
    68→def _build_import_load_config(
    69→    *,
    70→    lang_name: str | None,
    71→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,

    74→) -> import_helpers_mod.ImportLoadConfig:

    78→        trusted_assessment_source=import_config.trusted_assessment_source,

    86→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    87→    *,
    88→    attested_external: bool,
    89→    allow_partial: bool,
    90→    override_enabled: bool,
    91→    override_attest: str | None,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    94→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    95→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    96→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    97→        )
    98→    if attested_external and allow_partial:

   100→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
   101→        )
   102→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:

   105→        )
   106→    if override_enabled and (

   108→    ):

   112→def _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
   113→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
   114→    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
   115→    keys: set[str] = set()
   116→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
   117→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
   118→        if normalized:
   119→            keys.add(normalized)
   120→    return keys
   121→
   122→
   123→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   124→    state: dict,
   125→    *,
   126→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   127→) -> int:
   128→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
   129→    if not assessment_keys:
   130→        return 0
   131→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   132→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   133→        return 0
   134→
   135→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   136→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   137→    marked = 0
   138→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   139→        payload = store.get(key)
   140→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   141→            continue
   142→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
   143→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   144→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   145→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   146→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   147→        marked += 1
   148→    return marked
   149→
   150→
```

```
1→"""Scorecard dimension row helpers for the engine/planning layer.
     2→
     3→Provides ``scorecard_dimension_rows`` without importing app-layer modules.
     4→App-layer scorecard renderers import this to keep dependency direction clean.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import DIMENSIONS
    10→
    11→
    12→def scorecard_dimension_rows(
    13→    state: dict,
    14→    *,
    15→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    17→    """Return scorecard rows using canonical dimension ordering.
    18→
    19→    Tries ``prepare_scorecard_dimensions`` from the app layer when available
    20→    (deferred import to avoid hard engine -> app dependency), then falls back
    21→    to a simple mechanical-dimension listing.
    22→    """
    23→    if dim_scores is None:
    24→        dim_scores = (
    25→            state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
    26→        )
    27→        projected_state = state
    28→    else:
    29→        projected_state = dict(state)
    30→        projected_state["dimension_scores"] = dim_scores
    31→
    32→    # Try the full app-layer scorecard projection when it can be imported.
    33→    try:
    34→        from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    35→            prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    36→        )
    37→
    38→        rows = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(projected_state)
    39→        if rows:
    40→            return rows
    41→    except ImportError as exc:
    42→        _ = exc
    43→
    44→    # Fallback for synthetic/unit-test states without full scorecard context.
    45→    fallback_dim_scores = dim_scores or {}
    46→    if not isinstance(fallback_dim_scores, dict):
    47→        return []
    48→
    49→    mechanical_names = [dimension.name for dimension in DIMENSIONS]
    50→    fallback_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
    51→    for name in mechanical_names:
    52→        data = fallback_dim_scores.get(name)
    53→        if isinstance(data, dict):
    54→            fallback_rows.append((name, data))
    55→    fallback_rows.extend(
    56→        sorted(
    57→            [
    58→                (name, data)
    59→                for name, data in fallback_dim_scores.items()
    60→                if name not in mechanical_names and isinstance(data, dict)
    61→            ],
    62→            key=lambda item: item[0].lower(),
    63→        )
    64→    )
    65→    return fallback_rows
    66→
```

Perfect! Now I have a complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive analysis document:

## Dimension Naming Mismatch Investigation - Complete Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, I can now explain the agent's confusion and point you to the root causes and solutions.

### Problem Summary

The agent was comparing **review batch output files** (which use snake_case dimension IDs like `api_surface_coherence`) against **scorecard display names** (which use human-readable titles like "API coherence"), leading to false positives about "missing" dimensions when only the name convention differed.

---

### 1. Where Dimension Display Names Are Defined

**Primary source:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/base/subjective_dimensions.py` (lines 21-45)

```python
DISPLAY_NAMES: dict[str, str] = {
    "cross_module_architecture": "Cross-module arch",
    "api_surface_coherence": "API coherence",
    "high_level_elegance": "High elegance",
    # ... etc
}
```

This is the canonical mapping from snake_case dimension IDs (keys) to display names (values). It's used everywhere:
- **Imported by:** `metadata.py` (dimension metadata builder)
- **Imported by:** `metadata_legacy.py` (backward compatibility wrapper)
- **Imported by:** `engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (scorecard rendering)

**Secondary sources:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/dimensions/metadata.py` - Merges display names from dimension prompts
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/review_data/dimensions.json` - Default dimension list + prompts with optional metadata overrides (including `meta.display_name`)

---

### 2. Where Review Output Files Get Their Names

**File naming scheme:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/runner_packets.py` (line 180)

```python
output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
```

The batch result files are named **by batch index only** (`batch-1.raw.txt`, `batch-2.raw.txt`, etc.), not by dimension names at all. The **actual dimension data** goes inside the JSON payload in these files.

**Key files in the pipeline:**
- **Generated:** `prepare_run_artifacts()` in `runner_packets.py` creates the output file paths
- **Read/parsed by:** `collect_batch_results()` in `runner_parallel.py` extracts JSON from these files
- **Normalized by:** `batch/core.py` validates the payload structure

---

### 3. The Mapping Chain: Display Name ↔ Snake Case ID

Here's the complete flow showing where mappings happen:

#### **A. Scorecard Display → Dimension CLI Keys**
**File:** `engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (lines 19-28)

```python
def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
        if display.lower() == lowered:
            return key
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
```

This function is critical—it:
1. Takes a display name from the scorecard (e.g., "API coherence")
2. Normalizes whitespace and removes " (subjective)" suffix
3. **Looks it up in DISPLAY_NAMES to find the snake_case ID** (e.g., "api_surface_coherence")
4. Falls back to a regex-based conversion if not found

#### **B. Scorecard Rows with Dimension Metadata**
**File:** `app/commands/scan/reporting/dimensions.py` (lines 47-100)

```python
def scorecard_dimension_entries(state: dict, *, dim_scores: dict | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""
    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    subjective_by_name = {
        entry["name"]: entry
        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    }
    # ... for each row, populate:
    # "dimension_key": dim_key,     # snake_case ID from subjective_assessment
    # "cli_keys": cli_keys,          # derived from dimension_cli_key()
```

Each scorecard row includes **both**:
- `dimension_key`: the snake_case ID (e.g., `"api_surface_coherence"`)
- `cli_keys`: list of CLI-compatible dimension keys
- `name`: the display name (e.g., `"API coherence"`)

#### **C. Import Assessment Keys Normalization**
**File:** `app/commands/review/importing/cmd.py` (lines 112-120)

```python
def _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
    keys: set[str] = set()
    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
        if normalized:
            keys.add(normalized)
    return keys
```

When importing batch results, the system:
1. Takes whatever format the agent used (display name, snake_case, or mixed)
2. **Normalizes to snake_case via `normalize_dimension_name()`**
3. Stores under the snake_case key in `subjective_assessments` state

---

### 4. The Agent's Problem: Understanding the Confusion

The agent likely:
1. Extracted dimension names from batch result JSON files (which use snake_case IDs)
2. Tried to match them against scorecard display names (which are human-readable)
3. Concluded they were "missing" when they didn't match, not recognizing they were the same thing with different naming

**Example of the confusion:**
```
Batch result file contains:  {"assessments": {"api_surface_coherence": 78.5}}
Scorecard shows:             "API coherence" (98% complete)
Agent thinks:                "api_surface_coherence not found in scorecard!" ❌
```

---

### 5. Tooling Problem vs Agent Problem

**Is this a tooling problem?** PARTIALLY.

**The real issue:** There's **no direct reverse mapping** exposed for external consumers. The agent had to infer:
- The relationship between batch JSON dimension IDs and scorecard display names
- The normalization rules for dimension keys
- Which dimensions are in scope

**What's missing:** No single utility function says "given a dimension ID, return its display name and vice versa." The mapping is scattered across:
- `base/subjective_dimensions.py` (DISPLAY_NAMES)
- `engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (dimension_cli_key)
- `intelligence/review/dimensions/metadata.py` (get_dimension_metadata)

---

### 6. Key Code Locations Summary

| What | Where |
|------|-------|
| **Dimension ID → Display Name mapping** | `base/subjective_dimensions.py:21-45` (DISPLAY_NAMES dict) |
| **Display Name → Dimension ID mapping** | `engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py:19-28` (dimension_cli_key) |
| **Batch output filenames** | `app/commands/review/runner_packets.py:180` (batch-{idx+1}.raw.txt) |
| **Batch result payload structure** | `app/commands/review/batch/core.py:75-82` (BatchResultPayload TypedDict) |
| **Dimension metadata (with display_name, weight)** | `intelligence/review/dimensions/metadata.py:120-182` |
| **Scorecard row construction** | `app/commands/scan/reporting/dimensions.py:47-100` |
| **Normalization of dimension names** | `intelligence/review/dimensions/__init__.py` (normalize_dimension_name) |

---

### 7. Recommendations

**For the agent's current problem:**
1. Use `dimension_display_name(dimension_id)` from `intelligence/review/dimensions/metadata.py:216` to get the human name
2. Or use `get_dimension_metadata(dimension_id)["display_name"]` for the full metadata

**For future tooling:**
Consider adding a public utility function to the main API:
```python
# In intelligence/review/dimensions/__init__.py or base/subjective_dimensions.py
def dimension_id_to_display(dimension_id: str) -> str:
    """Convert snake_case dimension ID to display name."""
    from desloppify.base.subjective_dimensions import DISPLAY_NAMES
    return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dimension_id, dimension_id.replace("_", " ").title())

def display_to_dimension_id(display_name: str) -> str:
    """Convert display name to snake_case dimension ID."""
    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import dimension_cli_key
    return dimension_cli_key(display_name)
```

This would make the mapping explicit and discoverable for future agents.
